Europe: Up To Grabs! — prompty do generatora grafik

Po jednym gotowym prompcie na każdą kartę (187 łącznie). Każdy = polski opis sceny + akcent frakcji + kompozycja typu karty + wspólne „DNA stylu" (identyczne wszędzie → spójna seria) + proporcja karty. Kliknij dowolny prompt, aby skopiować.

Działa w Nano Banana Pro / Midjourney / Freepik. Sceny po polsku (silniki radzą sobie z PL), dyrektywy stylu po angielsku dla pewności.

Jak używać: wklej cały prompt do generatora. --ar 63:88 to proporcja klasycznej karty (prowincje 3:4, komponenty 1:1) — jeśli Twój silnik nie zna --ar, usuń go i ustaw format w UI.
Spójność serii — najważniejsze: sam prompt NIE wystarczy. Test na Gemini pokazał, że te same reguły dają cztery różne „ręce”. Żeby cała talia wyglądała jak od jednego rysownika:
  1. Wygeneruj jeden obrazek wzorcowy (master) i wybieraj tak długo, aż będzie dokładnie tym, czego chcesz.
  2. Do każdej kolejnej karty podepnij go jako referencję stylu — na Freepiku „Style reference”, w Midjourney --sref <url>. To robi 80% roboty.
  3. Nie zmieniaj modelu w trakcie serii — inny model = inny rysownik, nawet przy tym samym prompcie.
  4. Generuj partiami po ok. 10 i porównuj z masterem, zanim pójdziesz dalej.

RZYM — Zachodnie Cesarstwo (36)

POSTAĆ

CESARZ IDIOTA

Ilustracja (PL): Za duża korona zsuwa mu się na oczy, mapę trzyma do góry nogami, doradcy łapią się za głowy.
„CESARZ IDIOTA": Za duża korona zsuwa mu się na oczy, mapę trzyma do góry nogami, doradcy łapią się za głowy. — the Polish sentence above is a SCENE BRIEF telling you what to draw — never render it as words, and never depict a figure of speech literally — FRAMING: exactly ONE full-length standing character, whole body visible including feet, centered, filling about 85% of the frame height — imperial-Rome accent: rust-red & gold, laurels and eagles, sun-bleached marble, purple-edged togas — ONE SINGLE ILLUSTRATION STYLE, as if every card in this deck was drawn by the SAME artist in the same week — TECHNIQUE: flat folk woodblock-poster illustration — drawn as clean vector-like shapes, NOT a painting, NOT a sketch, NOT 3D, NOT photorealistic — LINework: every shape has a black ink outline of CONSTANT medium thickness; no thin-to-thick tapering, no sketchy or doubled lines — COLOUR: strictly FLAT fills — exactly two tones per colour (base + one darker shade). NO gradients, NO airbrush, NO blending, NO cross-hatching, NO engraving lines, NO texture inside shapes — PALETTE: use ONLY these — parchment #efe4cf, rust #8f2d22, burnt-orange #c07a1a, ochre-gold #b8860b, deep-brown #5a4a35, muted-green #2f6f3a, dark-violet #3a2a55, charcoal #2b2117; warm earthy tones dominate, at most 6 colours per image — FACES: stylized comic faces — large nose, small round eyes, one clear exaggerated expression; never realistic portraiture — TONE: dramatic-but-comedic historical parody (NOT historically accurate) — FULL BLEED: the illustration fills the entire canvas edge to edge and is cropped by the canvas itself — treat any border, frame, mount or paper margin as part of the artwork that must be painted over — high readability at small size — BACKGROUND: one single flat parchment tone #efe4cf with faint paper grain and nothing else — no scenery, no landscape, no horizon line, no props behind the subject — ABSOLUTELY NO TEXT ANYWHERE IN THE IMAGE: no title, no caption, no lettering, no letters, numbers, runes or symbols-as-writing — and no inscriptions, signage or engraved words on buildings, gates, banners, shields or scrolls; leave those surfaces blank. All typography is added later in InDesign — --ar 63:88

JESZCZE JEDNA KOHORTA

Ilustracja (PL): Kolejna kohorta wciska się w kadr, żołnierze pchają się ramię w ramię.
„JESZCZE JEDNA KOHORTA": Kolejna kohorta wciska się w kadr, żołnierze pchają się ramię w ramię. — the Polish sentence above is a SCENE BRIEF telling you what to draw — never render it as words, and never depict a figure of speech literally — FRAMING: ONE single iconic object centered, filling about 60% of the frame, no character — imperial-Rome accent: rust-red & gold, laurels and eagles, sun-bleached marble, purple-edged togas — ONE SINGLE ILLUSTRATION STYLE, as if every card in this deck was drawn by the SAME artist in the same week — TECHNIQUE: flat folk woodblock-poster illustration — drawn as clean vector-like shapes, NOT a painting, NOT a sketch, NOT 3D, NOT photorealistic — LINework: every shape has a black ink outline of CONSTANT medium thickness; no thin-to-thick tapering, no sketchy or doubled lines — COLOUR: strictly FLAT fills — exactly two tones per colour (base + one darker shade). NO gradients, NO airbrush, NO blending, NO cross-hatching, NO engraving lines, NO texture inside shapes — PALETTE: use ONLY these — parchment #efe4cf, rust #8f2d22, burnt-orange #c07a1a, ochre-gold #b8860b, deep-brown #5a4a35, muted-green #2f6f3a, dark-violet #3a2a55, charcoal #2b2117; warm earthy tones dominate, at most 6 colours per image — FACES: stylized comic faces — large nose, small round eyes, one clear exaggerated expression; never realistic portraiture — TONE: dramatic-but-comedic historical parody (NOT historically accurate) — FULL BLEED: the illustration fills the entire canvas edge to edge and is cropped by the canvas itself — treat any border, frame, mount or paper margin as part of the artwork that must be painted over — high readability at small size — BACKGROUND: one single flat parchment tone #efe4cf with faint paper grain and nothing else — no scenery, no landscape, no horizon line, no props behind the subject — ABSOLUTELY NO TEXT ANYWHERE IN THE IMAGE: no title, no caption, no lettering, no letters, numbers, runes or symbols-as-writing — and no inscriptions, signage or engraved words on buildings, gates, banners, shields or scrolls; leave those surfaces blank. „NAJLEPSZE KONIE W CAŁEJ GERMANII": Koń wygląda znacznie pewniej i mądrzej niż jego jeździec. — the Polish sentence above is a SCENE BRIEF telling you what to draw — never render it as words, and never depict a figure of speech literally — FRAMING: ONE single iconic object centered, filling about 60% of the frame, no character — Gothic accent: iron-grey & muted green, furs and horsehair, windswept motion, rough wooden shields — ONE SINGLE ILLUSTRATION STYLE, as if every card in this deck was drawn by the SAME artist in the same week — TECHNIQUE: flat folk woodblock-poster illustration — drawn as clean vector-like shapes, NOT a painting, NOT a sketch, NOT 3D, NOT photorealistic — LINework: every shape has a black ink outline of CONSTANT medium thickness; no thin-to-thick tapering, no sketchy or doubled lines — COLOUR: strictly FLAT fills — exactly two tones per colour (base + one darker shade). NO gradients, NO airbrush, NO blending, NO cross-hatching, NO engraving lines, NO texture inside shapes — PALETTE: use ONLY these — parchment #efe4cf, rust #8f2d22, burnt-orange #c07a1a, ochre-gold #b8860b, deep-brown #5a4a35, muted-green #2f6f3a, dark-violet #3a2a55, charcoal #2b2117; warm earthy tones dominate, at most 6 colours per image — FACES: stylized comic faces — large nose, small round eyes, one clear exaggerated expression; never realistic portraiture — TONE: dramatic-but-comedic historical parody (NOT historically accurate) — FULL BLEED: the illustration fills the entire canvas edge to edge and is cropped by the canvas itself — treat any border, frame, mount or paper margin as part of the artwork that must be painted over — high readability at small size — BACKGROUND: one single flat parchment tone #efe4cf with faint paper grain and nothing else — no scenery, no landscape, no horizon line, no props behind the subject — ABSOLUTELY NO TEXT ANYWHERE IN THE IMAGE: no title, no caption, no lettering, no letters, numbers, runes or symbols-as-writing — and no inscriptions, signage or engraved words on buildings, gates, banners, shields or scrolls; leave those surfaces blank. NO gradients, NO airbrush, NO blending, NO cross-hatching, NO engraving lines, NO texture inside shapes — PALETTE: use ONLY these — parchment #efe4cf, rust #8f2d22, burnt-orange #c07a1a, ochre-gold #b8860b, deep-brown #5a4a35, muted-green #2f6f3a, dark-violet #3a2a55, charcoal #2b2117; warm earthy tones dominate, at most 6 colours per image — FACES: stylized comic faces — large nose, small round eyes, one clear exaggerated expression; never realistic portraiture — TONE: dramatic-but-comedic historical parody (NOT historically accurate) — FULL BLEED: the illustration fills the entire canvas edge to edge and is cropped by the canvas itself — treat any border, frame, mount or paper margin as part of the artwork that must be painted over — high readability at small size — BACKGROUND: simple flat landscape built from large plain colour shapes — at most three depth layers, no fine detail, no clutter — ABSOLUTELY NO TEXT ANYWHERE IN THE IMAGE: no title, no caption, no lettering, no letters, numbers, runes or symbols-as-writing — and no inscriptions, signage or engraved words on buildings, gates, banners, shields or scrolls; leave those surfaces blank. Przy układaniu stołu nie może zdradzać, która to prowincja. — the Polish sentence above is a SCENE BRIEF telling you what to draw — never render it as words, and never depict a figure of speech literally — FRAMING: flat symmetrical graphic asset, centered, print-ready — game-component design asset, clean flat graphic, print-ready, minimal — ONE SINGLE ILLUSTRATION STYLE, as if every card in this deck was drawn by the SAME artist in the same week — TECHNIQUE: flat folk woodblock-poster illustration — drawn as clean vector-like shapes, NOT a painting, NOT a sketch, NOT 3D, NOT photorealistic — LINework: every shape has a black ink outline of CONSTANT medium thickness; no thin-to-thick tapering, no sketchy or doubled lines — COLOUR: strictly FLAT fills — exactly two tones per colour (base + one darker shade). NO gradients, NO airbrush, NO blending, NO cross-hatching, NO engraving lines, NO texture inside shapes — PALETTE: use ONLY these — parchment #efe4cf, rust #8f2d22, burnt-orange #c07a1a, ochre-gold #b8860b, deep-brown #5a4a35, muted-green #2f6f3a, dark-violet #3a2a55, charcoal #2b2117; warm earthy tones dominate, at most 6 colours per image — FACES: stylized comic faces — large nose, small round eyes, one clear exaggerated expression; never realistic portraiture — TONE: dramatic-but-comedic historical parody (NOT historically accurate) — FULL BLEED: the illustration fills the entire canvas edge to edge and is cropped by the canvas itself — treat any border, frame, mount or paper margin as part of the artwork that must be painted over — high readability at small size — BACKGROUND: one single flat parchment tone #efe4cf with faint paper grain and nothing else — no scenery, no landscape, no horizon line, no props behind the subject — ABSOLUTELY NO TEXT ANYWHERE IN THE IMAGE: no title, no caption, no lettering, no letters, numbers, runes or symbols-as-writing — and no inscriptions, signage or engraved words on buildings, gates, banners, shields or scrolls; leave those surfaces blank.
